\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}f\end{subarray}}\!\left[\bra{y_{1}}H_{f}^{\dagger}\ket{y_{1}}\bra{y^{*}_{1}}H_{f}\ket{\overline{y^{*}_{1}}}\right]=\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}f\end{subarray}}\!\left[\bra{\overline{y_{1}}}H_{f}^{\dagger}\ket{y_{1}}\bra{y^{*}_{1}}H_{f}\ket{y^{*}_{1}}\right]=0\kern 5.0pt.

·

Suppose
R\neq T
. Similarly, we have
\langle\phi_{L,R}|\phi_{S,T}\rangle=0
. ∎

0.C.2
Proof of
Lemma
13

Lemma
13
.

For two integers
l
and
r
such that
0\leq l+r\leq N
, and two relations

L=\left\{(x_{1},y_{1}),\dots,(x_{l},y_{l})\right\}\in\mathfrak{R}_{l}
and

R=\left\{(x^{\prime}_{1},y^{\prime}_{1}),\dots,(x^{\prime}_{r},y^{\prime}_{r})\right\}\in\mathfrak{R}_{r}
, we have for
x\in\{0,1\}^{n}

\mathsf{HPO}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{\phi_{L,R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-l-r}}\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{\phi_{L\cup\left\{x,y\right\},R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we have for
y\in\{0,1\}^{n}

\mathsf{HPO}^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{\phi_{L,R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-l-r}}\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{\phi_{L,R\cup\left\{x,y\right\}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

In the proof of
Lemma
6
, we know

\displaystyle\mathsf{HPO}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle={H_{f}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{\sigma(x)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n},b\in\{0,1\}}\langle y\rvert H_{f}\lvert y^{\oplus b}\rangle\delta_{y^{\oplus b}=\sigma(x)}\lvert y\rangle_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, for
\mathsf{HPO}^{\dagger}
we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{HPO}^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}=\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n},b\in\{0,1\}}\bra{y^{\oplus b}}H_{f}^{\dagger}\ket{y}\delta_{y^{\oplus b}=\sigma(x)}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{HPO}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{\phi_{L,R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{3d(n-1)}(N-l-r)!}}\sum_{f,\sigma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\{0,1\}^{l}\\
b^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{r}\end{subarray}}\prod_{i=1}^{l}\langle y_{i}\rvert H_{f}\lvert y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}\rangle\delta_{y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}=\sigma(x_{i})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\prod_{i=1}^{r}\langle{y^{\prime}}_{i}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}\rvert H_{f}^{\dagger}\lvert y^{\prime}_{i}\rangle\delta_{{y^{\prime}}_{i}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}=\sigma(x^{\prime}_{i})}\cdot\mathsf{HPO}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{3d(n-1)}(N-l-r)!}}\sum_{f,\sigma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\{0,1\}^{l}\\
b^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{r}\end{subarray}}\prod_{i=1}^{l}\langle y_{i}\rvert H_{f}\lvert y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}\rangle\delta_{y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}=\sigma(x_{i})}

\displaystyle\quad\prod_{i=1}^{r}\langle{y^{\prime}}_{i}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}\rvert H_{f}^{\dagger}\lvert y^{\prime}_{i}\rangle\delta_{{y^{\prime}}_{i}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}=\sigma(x^{\prime}_{i})}\cdot\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n},b\in\{0,1\}}\langle y\rvert H_{f}\lvert y^{\oplus b}\rangle\delta_{y^{\oplus b}=\sigma(x)}\lvert y\rangle_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-l-r}}\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\lvert y\rangle_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{\phi_{L\cup\left\{x,y\right\},R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{HPO}^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{\phi_{L,R}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{3d(n-1)}(N-l-r)!}}\sum_{f,\sigma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\{0,1\}^{l}\\
b^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{r}\end{subarray}}\prod_{i=1}^{l}\langle y_{i}\rvert H_{f}\lvert y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}\rangle\delta_{y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}=\sigma(x_{i})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\prod_{i=1}^{r}\langle{y^{\prime}_{i}}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}\rvert H_{f}^{\dagger}\lvert y^{\prime}_{i}\rangle\delta_{{y^{\prime}_{i}}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}=\sigma(x^{\prime}_{i})}\cdot\mathsf{HPO}^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AHP}}}\ket{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2^{3d(n-1)}(N-l-r)!}}\sum_{f,\sigma}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\{0,1\}^{l}\\
b^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{r}\end{subarray}}\prod_{i=1}^{l}\langle y_{i}\rvert H_{f}\lvert y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}\rangle\delta_{y_{i}^{\oplus b_{i}}=\sigma(x_{i})}

\displaystyle\quad\prod_{i=1}^{r}\langle{y^{\prime}_{i}}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}\rvert H_{f}^{\dagger}\lvert y^{\prime}_{i}\rangle\delta_{{y^{\prime}_{i}}^{\oplus b^{\prime}_{i}}=\sigma(x^{\prime}_{i})}\cdot\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n},b\in\{0,1\}}\langle y^{\oplus b}\rvert H_{f}^{\dagger}\lvert y\rangle\delta_{y^{\oplus b}=\sigma(x)}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{f}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{H}}}\ket{\sigma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle\,=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-l-r}}\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{\phi_{L,R\cup\left\{x,y\right\}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{HP}}}\kern 5.0pt.\qed

Appendix 0.D
Approximate Two-Side Unitary Invariance

We will show that the path-recording oracle
V
defined in
Definition
16

satisfies an approximate unitary invariance property in this section.
Formally, we have

Lemma
16
.

For any two
n
-qubit unitaries
C
and
D
, and any integer
0\leq t\leq N-1
,

\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt,

\left\lVert C^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot(V^{\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot D^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot(V^{\dagger})_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

This lemma is proved by showing the closeness between
V^{L}
and
E^{L}

as well as
V^{R}
and
E^{R}

where
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
are operators introduced
in
[
22
, Section 10.1]

that have exact two-side unitary invariance property.
We first give the definition of
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
.

Definition 25
.

E^{L}
and
E^{R}
are linear maps on register
\mathsf{A}
,
\mathsf{L}
and
\mathsf{R}
such that

\displaystyle E^{L}\coloneq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{y}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\sum_{L\in\mathfrak{R}}\sqrt{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)+1}\cdot\ket{L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{R\in\mathfrak{R}}\ket{R}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle E^{R}\coloneq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{y}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\sum_{L\in\mathfrak{R}}\ket{L}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{R\in\mathfrak{R}}\sqrt{\mathrm{num}\!\left(R,(x,y)\right)+1}\cdot\ket{R\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 4.62497pt.

Here,
\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)
is the number of times that
(x,y)
appears in
L
.

E^{L}
and
E^{R}
satisfy the exact unitary invariance:

Lemma 63

(Claim 20 in
[
22
]
)

For any two
n
-qubit unitaries
C
and
D
,

\displaystyle D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot E^{L}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}=Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot E^{L}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}^{\dagger}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot E^{R}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}=Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot E^{R}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}^{\dagger}\kern 5.0pt.

The approximate unitary invariance of
V
arises from the property that

V^{L}
and
V^{R}
are very close to
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
in operator norm respectively.
That is,

Lemma 64

For any integer
0\leq t\leq N-1
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert V^{L}_{\leq t}-E^{L}_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4t(t+1)}{N}}\quad\text{and}\quad\left\lVert V^{R}_{\leq t}-E^{R}_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We demonstrate that
V^{L}
and
E^{L}
are close in operator norm, and a similar argument shows that the proximity between
V^{R}
and
E^{R}
holds as well.
It is sufficient to show that for any state

\ket{\psi}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\ket{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\ket{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\ket{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,

we have

\left\lVert V^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}-E^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{4t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

Note that

\displaystyle V^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}-E^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\cdot\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\left(\frac{\delta_{y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)}}{\sqrt{N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}}-\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\ket{y}\ket{L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}}\ket{R}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\!\!\!\alpha_{x,L,R}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\ket{y}\ket{L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}}\ket{R}}_{\ket{u}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\!\!\!\alpha_{x,L,R}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left(-\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\ket{y}\ket{L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}}\ket{R}}_{\ket{v}}\,.

Since
\ket{u}
and
\ket{v}
are orthogonal, we have

\left\lVert V^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}-E^{L}_{\leq t}\ket{\psi}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}=\langle u|u\rangle+\langle v|v\rangle\kern 5.0pt.

Therefore, it is left to show
\langle u|u\rangle\leq\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}
and
\langle v|v\rangle\leq\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}
.

Notice that we can rewrite
\ket{u}
as

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\right)\ket{y}\ket{L^{\prime}}\ket{R}\kern 4.62497pt.

Thus, by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\langle u|u\rangle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\right)\cdot A_{y,L^{\prime},R}\kern 5.0pt,

where

\displaystyle A_{y,L^{\prime},R}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{2}\kern 5.0pt.

For
A_{y,L^{\prime},R}
, we have

\displaystyle A_{y,L^{\prime},R}=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N}-\sqrt{N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}}{\sqrt{N(N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|)}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}{N(N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{(t+1)(\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L^{\prime}\cup R\right)\right|-2)}{N(N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L^{\prime}\cup R\right)\right|+2)}\kern 5.0pt,

where the first inequality follows from the fact that, for nonnegative real numbers

a\geq b
, one has
\sqrt{a}-\sqrt{b}\leq\sqrt{a-b}
.
The second inequality uses the following observation: once
y,L^{\prime},R
are fixed,
the number of summands satisfying
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\}
and

y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)
is at most
t+1
, and

\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L^{\prime}\cup R\right)\right|=\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|+2.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\langle u|u\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\cdot\frac{(t+1)\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}{N(N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\cdot\left(\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\delta_{y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)}\right)\cdot\frac{(t+1)\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|}{N(N-\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{(t+1)2t}{N}\kern 5.0pt,

where the last inequality follows from

\left|\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\right|\leq 2t
.

As for
\ket{v}
, notice that we can rewrite it as

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\left(-\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\right)\ket{y}\ket{L^{\prime}}\ket{R}

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\langle v|v\rangle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\right)\cdot B_{y,L^{\prime},R}\kern 5.0pt,

where

B_{y,L^{\prime},R}\coloneqq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\frac{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L,(x,y)\right)+1}{N}\kern 5.0pt.

For
B_{y,L^{\prime},R}
, we have

\displaystyle B_{y,L^{\prime},R}=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\frac{\mathrm{num}\!\left(L^{\prime},(x,y)\right)}{N}\leq~\frac{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\mathrm{num}\!\left(L^{\prime},(x,y)\right)}{N}\leq~\frac{t+1}{N}\kern 5.0pt,

where the last inequality follows because, for fixed
y
,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\mathrm{num}\!\left(L^{\prime},(x,y)\right)
is precisely the number
of occurrences of
y
in
L^{\prime}
, which is at most
t+1
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\langle v|v\rangle\leq

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L^{\prime},R\in\mathfrak{R}:\left|L^{\prime}\cup R\right|\leq t+1\end{subarray}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n},L\in\mathfrak{R}:\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\left\{(x,y)\right\},\\
y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\right)\cdot\frac{t+1}{N}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\{0,1\}^{n}\\
L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\text{ s.t. }\left|L\cup R\right|\leq t\end{subarray}}\left|\alpha_{x,L,R}\right|^{2}\cdot\left(\sum_{y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\delta_{y\in\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)}\right)\cdot\frac{t+1}{N}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}\kern 5.0pt.\qed

Next, we prove the main lemma in this section.

Proof of
Lemma
16
.

To prove

\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt,

it is equivalent to show

\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

By the triangle inequality and expanding the definition of operator
V
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(a)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot\left(V^{L}\cdot V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(V^{L}\cdot V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(b)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot\left(V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(c)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot\left(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,{\dagger}}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,{\dagger}}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(d)}\kern 4.62497pt.

For
(a)
, by the unitary invariance of operator
E^{L}
(
Lemma
63
) and the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle(a)

\displaystyle\leq~\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot E^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\left\lVert Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot E^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq~2\left\lVert V^{L}_{\leq t}-E^{L}_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq~4\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

For
(b)
, notice that
\left(V^{L}\cdot V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}=V^{L}_{\leq t}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle(b)=

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\lVert\begin{aligned} &D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot C^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\\
&\quad-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\end{aligned}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert C^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt.

The first term is exactly
(a)
which is bounded by
4\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}
. As for the second one, note that
\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}=V^{R}_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}_{\leq t-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert C^{\dagger}\cdot_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}\right)_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\lVert C^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{R}_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}_{\leq t-1}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{R}_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert C^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{R}_{\leq t-1}\cdot D^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{R}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}_{\leq t-1}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V^{R,{\dagger}}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt,

where the last inequality follows from a similar argument as
(a)
. Then, we have
(b)\leq 12\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}
.
Similarly, we obtain
(c)\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}
and
(d)\leq 12\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}
. Thus,

\left\lVert D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

The other inequality in
Lemma
16
follows from a similar argument.
∎

Appendix 0.E
Proof of
Claim
5.8

We first need a twirling lemma.
Recall that

\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\coloneq\sum_{L,R:L\cup R\in\mathfrak{R}^{\mathrm{DB}}}\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,\kern 5.0pt\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\coloneq\sum_{L,R\in\mathfrak{R}}\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Lemma 65

(Twirling)

Let
\mathcal{D}\in\left\{\mathcal{D}_{1},\mathcal{D}_{2}\right\}
as defined in
Definition
18
.
For an integer
0\leq t\leq N/4
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 16t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 16t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt.

The proof of this twirling lemma is deferred to

Appendix
0.F
.
Now, we proceed to prove
Claim
5.8
.

Claim
5.8
.

For any integer
t\geq 0
and
\mathcal{D}\in\left\{\mathcal{D}_{1},\mathcal{D}_{2}\right\}
as defined in
Definition
18

\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLRCD}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LRCD}}}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}\right)\right]\geq 1-\frac{38t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof by induction.

For the base case
t=0
,
by
Definition
19
,
Definition

20
and
Definition

21
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LRCD}}}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{V}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cQ}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LRCD}}}\cdot\left(\ket{0^{n}0^{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AB}}}\ket{\left\{\right\}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\ket{\left\{\right\}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\ket{0^{n}0^{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{AB}}}\ket{\left\{\right\}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\ket{\left\{\right\}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}

\displaystyle=\ket{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLRCD}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Thus, the base case holds:

\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLRCD}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LRCD}}}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{V}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}\right)\right]=1
.

Assuming the claim holds for some
t\geq 0
, we now
prove it for
t+1
. Since the argument is symmetric,
we assume the
(t+1)
-th query is a forward query, i.e.

b_{t+1}=0
, without loss of generality. Thus,

\displaystyle\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLRCD}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC}\right)\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LRCD}}}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{V}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{CD}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\left(V\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right)

which gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right)\right]\\
&=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\left(V\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right)\right]\\
&=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right],\end{split}

(31)

because of
Fact
5.7
. Recall the notation
W_{\leq t}=W\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
and
V_{\leq t}=V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
. Then, via rewriting

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}+\big(\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}-\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\big)\kern 5.0pt,

we further rewrite
(\ref{clm18-eq1})=(*)+(**)
where

\displaystyle(*)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right]\,,

\displaystyle(**)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\big(\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}-\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\big)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right]\,.

We first lower bound
(**)
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}(**)&\geq-\left\lVert\big(\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}-\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}\big)\right\rVert_{\infty}\\
&\geq-\left\lVert\sum_{C,D}\big(D_{A}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{A}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\big)\otimes\ket{C,D}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{C,D}\right\rVert_{\infty}\\
&\geq-\max_{C,D}{\left\lVert D_{A}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot C_{A}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot V_{\leq t}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\\
&\geq-32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.\end{split}

The first inequality follows from
Fact
5.6
, ensuring that the norm of

\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}

and
A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}
are at most
1
.
The last inequality follows from
Lemma
16
.
To bound
(*)
, we first utilize the properties of
W
and
V
to derive:

\displaystyle\begin{split}W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot V_{\leq t}&=\left(W\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\right)^{\dagger}\cdot V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\\
&=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot W^{\dagger}\cdot V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\\
&=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\\
&=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}-\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}\right)\right)\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\\
&=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\kern 5.0pt.\end{split}

(32)

Then, we can rewrite
(*)=(\triangle)-(\triangle\triangle)
using
Eq.

32
where:

\displaystyle(\triangle)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right]\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle(\triangle\triangle)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right].

Thus, to bound
(*)
we need to separately bound
(\triangle)
and
(\triangle\triangle)
. First, in
(\triangle)
we have:

\displaystyle\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\kern 5.0pt.

Thus, by the inductive hypothesis, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\triangle)&=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right]\\
&=\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right]\\
&\geq 1-\frac{38t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}\kern 5.0pt.\end{split}

Then we will upper-bound
(\triangle\triangle)
:

\displaystyle(\triangle\triangle)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{u}\in\mathcal{H}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLRCD}}}:\left\lVert\ket{u}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1\\
\ket{v}\in\mathcal{H}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}:\left\lVert\ket{v}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\left|\bra{u}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\ket{v}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right|

\displaystyle=\left(\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{v}\in\mathcal{H}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ABLR}}}:\\
\left\lVert\ket{v}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\bra{v}\bra{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\ket{v}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t}\right)\cdot\left(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(16t\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\right)^{1/2}\leq\frac{6t^{3/4}}{N^{1/4}}\kern 4.62497pt,

where the last line follows from
Lemma
65
.

Now, putting everything together we show the claim for
t+1
to conclude:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\left[\bra{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{W,\mathcal{D}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\left(\ket{\mathcal{A}_{t+1}^{V}}\ket{\mathsf{init}(\mathcal{D})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=(*)+(**)

\displaystyle\geq(*)-32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}

\displaystyle\geq(\triangle)-(\triangle\triangle)-32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{38t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}-\frac{6t^{3/4}}{N^{1/4}}-32\sqrt{\frac{t(t+1)}{N}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{1}{N^{1/4}}\left(38t^{2}+6t^{3/4}+32\frac{\sqrt{t(t+1)}}{N^{1/4}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{1}{N^{1/4}}\left(38t^{2}+6t+32(t+1)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{38(t+1)^{2}}{N^{1/4}}\kern 5.0pt.\qed

Appendix 0.F
A Twirling Lemma

In this section, we prove
Lemma
65
. We first analyze
\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
and
\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
and then give the proof.

0.F.1
Properties of
\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}
and
\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}

We need some notation:

\displaystyle\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\\
x\notin\mathrm{BDom}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Im}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}L,R\in\mathfrak{R}\\
y\notin\mathrm{BIm}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\ket{y}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{x,y\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{y,y}\kern 5.0pt.

We have the following lemma:

Lemma 66

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Im}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 4.62497pt.

Here,

\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
denotes the operator that acts on
\mathsf{A}
and
\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}
such that
it applies
\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}
to
\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}
,
while applying the identity to the remainder of
\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}
.

Proof.

We show the first equality, and the other one follows from a similar argument.
Note that
\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}=\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W^{L}\right)}+\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W^{R}\right)}
. So, it suffices to show

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W^{L}\right)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W^{R}\right)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

It is easy to see that

\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W^{L}\right)}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}L\cup R\in\mathfrak{R}^{\mathrm{DB}}\\
x\notin\mathrm{Dom}\left(L\cup R\right)\end{subarray}}\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

We now turn to proving the second equality. By definition of
W^{R}
, we know

\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W^{R}\right)}=W^{R}\cdot W^{R,{\dagger}}=W^{R}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0,\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\Pi_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot W^{R,{\dagger}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0,\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}W^{R}_{l,r}\cdot W^{R,{\dagger}}_{l,r}\kern 5.0pt.

Therefore, we are left to show

W^{R}_{l,r}\cdot W^{R,{\dagger}}_{l,r}=\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Recall the operator
E^{R}
in
Definition
25
. It is not hard to check

W^{R}_{l,r}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{\sqrt{N-2l-2r}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot E^{R}_{l,r}\kern 5.0pt.

Therefore,

\displaystyle W^{R}_{l,r}\cdot W^{R,{\dagger}}_{l,r}

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot E^{R}_{l,r}\cdot E^{R,{\dagger}}_{l,r}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{l,r+1,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt,

where the second inequality is from
[
22
, Eq. (11.26)]
.
∎

For
l,r\geq 0
, we define

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{L,R\in\mathfrak{R}}\ket{L}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{L}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{R}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{R}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x_{1},\cdots,x_{l},x^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,x^{\prime}_{l})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r}\\
(y_{1},\cdots,y_{l},y^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,y^{\prime}_{l})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r}\end{subarray}}\ket{x_{1},\cdots,x_{l},y_{1},\cdots,y_{l}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x_{1},\cdots,x_{l},y_{1},\cdots,y_{l}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\otimes\ket{x^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,x^{\prime}_{r},y^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,y^{\prime}_{r}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,x^{\prime}_{r},y^{\prime}_{1},\cdots,y^{\prime}_{r}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r\leq N\end{subarray}}\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

It is evident that

\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}=\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}=\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Now we define

\displaystyle J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle J^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\coloneq\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Im}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r<N/2\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-2l-2r}\cdot\Pi_{l,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 4.62497pt.

Then we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot J^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

We will need the following lemma when proving
Lemma
65
.

Lemma 67

For non-negative
l,r
such that
l+r<N/2
,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\preceq\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+2\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\cdot\mathds{1}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\right)\kern 5.0pt,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle-J^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\preceq\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\right)+2\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\cdot\mathds{1}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\right)\kern 5.0pt,

where

\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}=\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}\otimes\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}\kern 5.0pt,\kern 5.0pt\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}=\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}\otimes\ket{\bar{x}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{\bar{x}}\kern 5.0pt.

Proof.

We prove the first inequality, and the other one follows from a similar argument.
Notice that

J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}=\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\notin\mathrm{Dom}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}-\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\preceq~

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.-~\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Since
\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}
,
\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}
and
\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r}
commute with each other,
we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-

\displaystyle J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\preceq~

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\preceq~

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle\preceq~

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+\lambda\cdot\mathds{1}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\right)

\displaystyle\preceq~

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+\lambda\cdot\mathds{1}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{ALR}}}\right)\kern 5.0pt,

where

\lambda\coloneqq\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt,

and

\Delta_{i}\coloneq\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}
.
Thus, we are left to show that
\lambda
is bounded by
2\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}
.
Notice that

\displaystyle\lambda~

\displaystyle\leq~\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ +~\left\lVert\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=~\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}^{2}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ +~\left\lVert\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}^{2}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq~2\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Delta_{i}-\Delta_{i}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=~2\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt.

So, it suffices to prove that

\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.

If
r=0
, this holds trivially. From now on, we assume
r\geq 1
.
Note that
\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}

is

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r-1}\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r}\end{subarray}}\ket{x,y}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}\backslash\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\otimes\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Here,
x\in\{0,1\}^{nl}
,
x^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{n(r-1)}
,
y\in\{0,1\}^{nl},\ y^{\prime}\in\{0,1\}^{nr}
and
T_{x,x^{\prime}}
denotes the set of all binary strings that do not belong to the same block as any string in
x
and
x^{\prime}
.
Similarly,
\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
is

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r-1}\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r}\end{subarray}}\ket{x,y}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}\backslash\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\otimes\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\kern 5.0pt.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r-1}\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathsf{DB}_{l+r}\end{subarray}}\ket{x,y}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{L}}}\otimes\ket{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{R}\backslash\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\otimes\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\kern 5.0pt.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\max_{x,x^{\prime}}~\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
w\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
z\in\{0,1\}^{n}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
w\notin T_{x,x^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}w\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}\\
z\notin T_{x,x^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\ket{z,z}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{w,w}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{(N-2l-2r+2)(2l+2r-2)}}{N}\left\lVert\ket{\phi}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{\psi}-\ket{\psi}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{\phi}\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt,

where

\displaystyle\ket{\phi}\coloneq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-2l-2r+2}}\sum_{z\in T_{x,x^{\prime}}}\ket{z,z}\kern 5.0pt\text{and}\kern 5.0pt\ket{\psi}\coloneq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2l+2r-2}}\sum_{z\notin T_{x,x^{\prime}}}\ket{z,z}\kern 5.0pt.

Since
\ket{\phi}
and
\ket{\psi}
are orthogonal, it is not hard to see that
\left\lVert\ket{\phi}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{\psi}-\ket{\psi}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{\phi}\right\rVert_{\infty}=1
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{(N-2l-2r+2)(2l+2r-2)}}{N}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.\qed

0.F.2
Proof of
Lemma
65

We need the following property of twirling with Haar random unitary.

Lemma 68

(Claim 2 in
[
22
]
)

Let
\mathcal{D}
be the Haar measure over
\mathrm{U}\!\left(N\right)
. We have

\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left(U\otimes\overline{U}\right)^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}\cdot\left(U\otimes\overline{U}\right)\right]=\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}+\frac{1}{N+1}\left(\mathds{1}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}\right)\kern 5.0pt.

Before proving
Lemma
65
, we prove a lemma that will assist in our argument.

Lemma 69

Let
\mathcal{D}\in\left\{\mathcal{D}_{1},\mathcal{D}_{2}\right\}
as defined in
Definition
18
. We have for non-negative
l,r

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\leq\frac{4l+r}{N-1}+\frac{7rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\leq\frac{l+4r}{N-1}+\frac{7lN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt.

Proof.

We show the first inequality for
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{2}
and the rest inequalities follow from a similar argument. From
Lemma
67
and the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[l]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(l)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{ffb}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\frac{N}{N-2l-2r+2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\cdot\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\frac{2rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ l\cdot\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\underbrace{\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes C)^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(C\otimes C)\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(1)}

\displaystyle+\ l\cdot\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\underbrace{\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes C)^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,\bar{x}}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x,\bar{x}}\cdot(C\otimes C)\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(2)}

\displaystyle+\ r\cdot\sum_{x\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\underbrace{\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes\overline{C})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,\bar{x}}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x,\bar{x}}\cdot(C\otimes\overline{C})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(3)}

\displaystyle+\frac{rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\underbrace{\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes\overline{C})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}\right)\cdot(C\otimes\overline{C})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}}_{(4)}

\displaystyle+\frac{2rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt.

For (1), notice that
C^{\dagger}
is equivalent to a
T
-step parallel Kac’s walk followed by an independent random permutation, which means
C^{\dagger}
is drawn from a
\frac{1}{N^{2}}-\textup{{RSS}}
distribution.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle(1)\leq\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mu\end{subarray}}\!\left[(U\otimes U)\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(U\otimes U)^{\dagger}\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}=\frac{2}{N(N+1)}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

For (2), notice that
C^{\dagger}=C^{\prime{\dagger}}\cdot P^{\dagger}
. So,

\displaystyle(2)\leq\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\end{subarray}}\!\left[(P\otimes P)^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,\bar{x}}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,\bar{x}}\cdot(P\otimes P)\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{1}{N(N-1)}\sum_{x\neq y}\ket{x,y}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,y}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=~\frac{1}{N(N-1)}\kern 5.0pt.

Similarly, we have
(3)\leq\frac{1}{N(N-1)}
.

We now give an upper bound on (4). We first prove that for any
x\in\{0,1\}^{n}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes\overline{C})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(C\otimes\overline{C})\right]-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mu\end{subarray}}\!\left[(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.6999pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\frac{4}{N^{2}}\kern 4.62497pt.

(33)

Notice that
C^{\dagger}
is equivalent to a
T
-step parallel Kac’s walk followed by a random permutation
P^{\prime}
.
Let
\ket{u}
be the random state after
T
steps of parallel Kac’s walk starting at
\ket{x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x}
, and
\ket{v}
be the Haar random state. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes\overline{C})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(C\otimes\overline{C})\right]-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mu\end{subarray}}\!\left[(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C\otimes\overline{C})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(C\otimes\overline{C})\right]-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mu\end{subarray}}\!\left[(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\ket{x,x}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{x,x}\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})\right]\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P^{\prime},\ket{u}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left(P^{\prime}\otimes\overline{P^{\prime}}\right)\left(\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}\right)\left(P^{\prime}\otimes\overline{P^{\prime}}\right)^{\dagger}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P^{\prime},\ket{v}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left(P^{\prime}\otimes\overline{P^{\prime}}\right)\left(\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right)\left(P^{\prime}\otimes\overline{P^{\prime}}\right)^{\dagger}\right]\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{u}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}\right]-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{v}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right]\right\rVert_{1}\kern 5.0pt.

From
[
21
, Theorem 4]
, we know that there is a joint distribution
\gamma
among
\ket{u}
and
\ket{v}
such that

\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{u}-\ket{v}\right\rVert_{2}\right]\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{u}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}\right]-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket{v}\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right]\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right]\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}\right]\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right]\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}\right\rVert_{1}\right]+\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\otimes\overline{\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}}\right\rVert_{1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\cdot\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{u}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{u}-\ket{v}\hskip-3.99994pt\bra{v}\right\rVert_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\cdot\left(\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{u}(\bra{u}-\bra{v})\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert(\ket{u}-\ket{v})\bra{v}\right\rVert_{1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\cdot\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma\end{subarray}}\!\left[\left\lVert\ket{u}-\ket{v}\right\rVert_{2}\right]\leq\frac{4}{N^{2}}\kern 5.0pt.

This establishes Eq.
(
33
)
.
From Eq.
(
33
)
,
Lemma
68
and the fact that
(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})=\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}
for any unitary
U
, we have

\displaystyle(4)\leq\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}U\leftarrow\mu\end{subarray}}\!\left[(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{EPR}}\right)\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}+\frac{4}{N}\leq\frac{1}{N+1}+\frac{4}{N}\leq\frac{5}{N}\kern 5.0pt.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}_{2}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{2l}{N+1}+\frac{l}{N}+\frac{l}{N-1}+\frac{r}{N-1}+\frac{5r}{N-2l-2r+2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{2rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{4l+r}{N-1}+\frac{7rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}\kern 5.0pt.\qed

We are now ready to prove
Lemma
65
. We restate the lemma here.

Lemma
65
.

For an integer
0\leq t\leq N/4
and

\mathcal{D}\in\left\{\mathcal{D}_{1},\mathcal{D}_{2}\right\}
as defined in
Definition
18
,
we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 16t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Im}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(D_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}^{\dagger}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 16t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\kern 4.62497pt.

Proof.

We prove the first inequality and the other follows from a similar argument.
Note that

\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}=\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\kern 5.0pt,

and
\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}
commutes with
Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
since
\Pi^{\mathfrak{R}^{2}}
is the sum of projectors onto the symmetric subspaces.
We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r\leq t\end{subarray}}\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{l,r,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}\kern 5.0pt,

where the equality holds because
\Pi^{\mathrm{db}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
and
J^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}
are block diagonal with respect to
l
and
r
. Then from
Lemma
69
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}C,D\leftarrow\mathcal{D}\end{subarray}}\!\left[(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\left(\Pi^{\mathrm{DB}}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{Dom}\left(W\right)}_{\leq t,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}}\right)\cdot(C_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{A}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{LR}}})\right]\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r\leq t\end{subarray}}~\frac{4l+r}{N-1}+\frac{7rN}{N-2l-2r+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2(l+r)}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,r\geq 0\\
l+r\leq t\end{subarray}}~\frac{3l+t}{N-1}+\frac{7rN}{N-2t+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{t}{N-1}+\frac{7tN}{N-2t+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\leq\frac{8tN}{N-2t+2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\leq 16t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t}{N}}\kern 5.0pt,

where the second inequality is from
l+r\leq t
, the third inequality holds since the maximal value is achieved at
r=t
, and the last one is from
t\leq N/4
.
∎
